- **quorum-analyze**: Stage 1b. The ticket path's counterpart to the PRD — reads the ticket, records what it asks, which surfaces it touches, and the acceptance criteria it already carries. - **quorum-architecture**: Stage 3 of the governed pipeline. Produces architecture.md — components touched, data and contract deltas, boundaries, failure modes and rollback. Invoked by /quorum-orchestrate; reads the profile for every stack fact. ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/commands/quorum-architecture.md) · ★1)
- **quorum-deliver**: Stage 9, the last one. Opens the PR with the accepted conditions in its body, posts the QA handoff, updates the tracker. Runs only on a VERIFIED-family verdict and never automatically — delivery acts outward. ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/commands/quorum-deliver.md) · ★1)
- **quorum-design**: Stage 4 of the governed pipeline. Produces design.md — flows, complete contracts, schema and indexes, edge cases and states. Invoked by /quorum-orchestrate; reads the profile for every stack fact. ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/commands/quorum-design.md) · ★1)
- **quorum-impl-audit**: The second audit gate. Judges the implementation against the approved scope, with the build/test/lint gate run first — a red gate forbids a clean verdict. Convenes a critic panel over the actual diff, and either clears the unit to deliver or emits a proposal. ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/commands/quorum-impl-audit.md) · ★1)
- **quorum-implement**: The implementation stage of the governed pipeline. Runs ONLY on a unit whose scope has been audited READY. Drives implementation → tests → code review → PR artifacts through gates, then hands back at IN_PROGRESS for the implementation audit. Invoked by /quorum-orchestrate; not an entry point. ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/commands/quorum-implement.md) · ★1)
- **quorum-orchestrate**: The single entry point for the governed pipeline. Reads .claude/specs/&lt;slug&gt;/status.yml and routes to the next stage — triage, product shaping, audits, implementation, delivery. Drives one unit, or every eligible unit in one session with --queue. ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/commands/quorum-orchestrate.md) · ★1)
- **quorum-pr-template**: Generate a PR description linking the ticket, investigation prompt, validation report, and run commands. Stack-agnostic — works for any artifact type (code changes, test files, configs). ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/commands/quorum-pr-template.md) · ★1)
- **quorum-prd**: Stage 1a. Shapes the problem — problem, users, value, success, non-goals, the constitution articles it touches — by interviewing the operator. Always human-interactive; the transition to PRD_READY is the agent frontier and only a human crosses it. ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/commands/quorum-prd.md) · ★1)
- **quorum-prompt-gen**: Generate a structured investigation prompt for a ticket. Fetches the ticket, analyzes requirements, recommends memory bank patterns, and produces a stack-agnostic implementation/test-authoring guide. ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/commands/quorum-prompt-gen.md) · ★1)
- **quorum-scope-audit**: The first audit gate. Judges whether a unit's scope is sound and traceable enough to implement — before any code is written. Convenes a critic panel, walks the constitution, and either clears the unit to implement or emits a proposal naming the stage the fix belongs to. ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/commands/quorum-scope-audit.md) · ★1)
- **quorum-spec**: Stage 2 of the governed pipeline. Produces requirements.md — goals, scope, non-goals, constraints and acceptance criteria with stable ids. Invoked by /quorum-orchestrate; reads the profile for every stack fact. ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/commands/quorum-spec.md) · ★1)
- **quorum-tasks**: Stage 5 of the governed pipeline. Produces tasks.md — an ordered task list where every task carries its file set and cites an acceptance criterion. Invoked by /quorum-orchestrate; reads the profile for every stack fact. ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/commands/quorum-tasks.md) · ★1)
- **quorum-triage**: Stage 0 of the governed pipeline. Reads the ticket or the stated scope and decides how the work enters — the spec path when the problem is still open, the ticket path when it is bounded — and how deep the product stages go. Creates status.yml with origin and complexity. ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/commands/quorum-triage.md) · ★1)
- **quorum-validate-ticket**: Validate work artifacts (code changes, tests) against a ticket's acceptance criteria. ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/commands/quorum-validate-ticket.md) · ★1)
